  - **Decisions & notes:** Performance benchmarks use in-memory implementations (`InMemoryIndexManager`, `InMemoryBatchWriter`, `InMemoryStatementCache`, `FakeSecurityScanner`) to avoid platform dependencies; all timing assertions use generous thresholds (<100ms for single operations, <200ms for batch operations) to accommodate CI environment variability; the `StatementCacheStats.hitRatio` returns `NaN` when no requests have been made (division by zero guard); the `ShardRouter` uses top-level `defaultShards` map (not static member); `CdnConfig` and `ConnectionPoolConfig` use named constructors (conservative/aggressive/loadTest) not static getters; **PHASE 13 COMPLETE** — all 5 tasks (13.1–13.5) done

### 2026-08-19 — Task 11.3: Rate Limiting & Abuse Prevention

- **Completed Phase 11.3: Rate Limiting & Abuse Prevention (Security Hardening & Compliance)**
  - **Domain layer (`lib/rate_limit/domain/`):** `rate_limit_policy.dart` (8 fixed enum policies: otpRequest/loginAttempt/usernameClaim/connectionRequest/postCreation/voteAction/apiMutation/generalAction — maxRequests + windowSeconds + cooldownSeconds + strict fromWireName decode, unknown throws FormatException) + `rate_limit_bucket.dart` (immutable entity: policy + requestCount + windowStart + cooldownActive + cooldownStartedAt; withRequest/withReset immutable operations returning new copies; isLimitReached/isWindowExpired/isCooldownExpired/remainingRequests/cooldownRemainingSeconds pure methods) + `abuse_trigger.dart` (8 AbuseTrigger types with AbuseSeverity levels + AbuseEvent entity: eventId + trigger + detectedAt + occurrenceCount — zero identity fields) + `rate_limit_repository.dart` (port: getBucket/recordRequest/getAbuseEvents/recordAbuseEvent/getAbuseEventCount — no identity, no PII)
  - **Data layer (`lib/rate_limit/data/`):** `in_memory_rate_limit_repository.dart` (in-memory impl with per-policy bucket tracking + abuse event storage) + `rate_limit_bucket_codec.dart` (SQLCipher row encode/decode with strict read-path revalidation — unknown policies rejected, missing columns throw FormatException)
  - **Schema v19 (`lib/database/domain/schema.dart`):** `rate_limit_buckets` (policy PK TEXT + request_count INTEGER + window_start INTEGER + cooldown_active INTEGER + cooldown_started_at INTEGER — ZERO identity columns) + `abuse_events` (event_id PK TEXT + trigger_type TEXT + severity TEXT + detected_at INTEGER + occurrence_count INTEGER — ZERO identity columns) + migration v19 (up: 2 CREATE TABLEs; down: 2 DROPs) + schema/migration tests updated (26 tables, fresh+incremental counts, v19 rollback)

  - VERIFY PASSED (unit): **74 new Flutter tests** — 12 policy (enum-count/wire-round-trip/labels-non-empty/unknown-throws/empty-throws/all-positive-maxRequests/all-positive-windowSeconds) + 12 bucket (initial-state/withRequest-increments/cooldown-activates/window-expired-reset/cooldown-reset/clears-cooldown/cooldownRemaining-zero/cooldownRemaining-correct/equality-by-policy-and-count/isWindowExpired-true/isWindowExpired-false/isCooldownExpired-true) + 22 abuse trigger (enum-count/8-wire-round-trip/labels-non-empty/severity-assigned/equality-by-eventId/hashCode-by-eventId) + 10 repo (getBucket-default/recordRequest-increments/cooldown-activates/independent-policies/getAbuseEvents-empty/getAbuseEvents-filter/recordAbuseEvent/getAbuseEventCount/clear) + 7 codec (encode-correct/round-trip/cooldown-active/missing-columns-throws/unknown-policy-throws/null-cooldown/all-policies-round-trip) + 6 state (default-idle/bucketFor-unknown/bucketFor-known/cooldownActive-false/cooldownActive-true/error-message) + 7 bloc (initial-idle/refresh-ready/load-existing/recordRequest-increments/recordRequest-limit/stream-emits/error-handling) + 5 widget (renders-title/second-policy/refresh-button/FLAG_SECURE/renders-without-errors) + 8 security-checkpoint (no-networking-domain/no-networking-data/no-print/no-print-state/no-identity-fields/schema-zero-identity/FLAG_SECURE/no-networking-UI) + schema +2 table-count/rate_limit+abuse_zero-identity-columns + migration +2 v19-upgrade/v19-rollback + harness smoke 11-tab Limits destination; **2064 total Flutter tests**, all green; `flutter analyze` 0 issues, `dart format` clean, affected suites 160/160 green

  - **Decisions & notes:** The rate limiter is CLIENT-side and local-first: buckets track request counts within sliding windows and activate cooldowns when limits are exceeded; the cooldown uses immutable bucket operations (withRequest/withReset) for thread safety; the 8 policies cover the full abuse surface (OTP bombing, brute force, username squatting, connection spam, post spam, vote manipulation, API abuse, general throttling); CAPTCHA challenge delivery is deferred with the Phase-4 infra surface; the harness 11th Limits tab provides immediate UI testing surface; the `isCooldownExpired` method only triggers a reset when the cooldown is actually active (preventing false resets on idle buckets)

### 2026-08-18 — Task 10.2: Civic Karma Engine

**Domain** — new `lib/karma/domain/` tree: `karma_action.dart` — `KarmaPillar` (ledger/warRoom/academy/crossPillar, strict wire decode — unknown throws) + `KarmaAction` implementing the **full PRD §9.2 action table** with FIXED deterministic deltas: `ledgerPostVerified` +5 (Ledger), `warRoomCaseContribution` +15 (War Room), `sandboxNoteUpvoted3` +3 (Academy), `academyModuleCompleted` +2 (Academy), `warRoomAnalystVetted` +20 (War Room, **one-time**), `ledgerPostRejected` −3 (Ledger), `confirmedAbuseReport` −25 (Cross-pillar) — each with a fixed non-PII `label` + `wireName` + strict `fromWireName` (a corrupt/forged row can never masquerade as a real action). `karma_event.dart` — `KarmaEvent`, the **APPEND-ONLY, AUDITABLE** ledger unit: monotonic `seq` + minted UUID v4 `eventId` (the wire `Idempotency-Key`) + **validated 64-hex `actorHash`** (the ONLY actor identifier, never rendered) + action + delta + running `balanceAfter` + timestamp + **`prevHash`/`selfHash` SHA-256 chain links** over the canonical bytes (same hash-chain scheme as the Task 8.6 custody log) + `Sha256Hasher` port. `karma_gate.dart` — `KarmaGate` (PRD §9.2 privileges): `skipProbationPosting` ≥50, `peerReviewVoting` ≥100, `warRoomAnalystEligibility` ≥150, `moderatorCouncil` ≥500 **AND 90-day tenure** (the only tenure-gated one); pure `isSatisfied(karma, accountAgeDays)`. `karma_calculation.dart` — `KarmaVoteWeight` SUB-LINEAR per-voter weight: `min(10, 1 + floor(sqrt(karma)))` — every additional karma adds less weight (deterministic, floored at 1 so new accounts still vote). `karma_decay.dart` — `KarmaDecay` −2%/month INACTIVE-account decay via **pure integer math** (`score * 98 ~/ 100` per month — no floats, deterministic across devices). `lockstep_detector.dart` — `KarmaVote` + `LockstepDetector` ANOMALY DETECTION (PRD §9.2 Sybil resistance WITHOUT banning): a cluster of **≥3 NEW accounts** (age < 30 days) all voting within a **10-minute window** dampens the tally weight to **25%** (`dampeningFactor`); established accounts never dampen; deterministic regardless of input order. `karma_repository.dart` port (`balance`/`events`/`record`/`verifyIntegrity` — **append-only, no update/delete API**) + `karma_event_source.dart` port for INBOUND events (the NATS JetStream consumer stays deferred with the Phase-4 infra surface; the client seam models where server-confirmed events land).

**Data & Schema** — `lib/karma/data/`: `karma_event_records.dart` (`KarmaEventRecord` row + strict `tryParse` READ-PATH revalidation — the delta MUST equal the action's fixed value, hashes must be 64-hex, unknown action wire rejected) + `local_karma_repository.dart` (`LocalKarmaRepository` over the SQLCipher `EntityStore` — OFFLINE-FIRST: every event lands in the encrypted partition immediately; the balance is ALWAYS the chain's last `balanceAfter` — never a separately-mutated counter — so the ledger stays auditable end-to-end; `record` enforces exactly-next-seq + prevHash-link or THROWS; `verifyIntegrity` recomputes every selfHash over the canonical bytes and walks the chain — a mutated delta, a reordered pair, and a removed event are all detected; `RealKarmaSha256Hasher` over the `cryptography` package) + `in_memory_karma_event_source.dart`. Schema **v14** — `karma_events` (`event_id` PK UUID, `seq`, `actor_hash` **SENSITIVE** (the blinded 64-hex actor), `action`, `delta`, `balance_after`, `occurred_at`, `prev_hash`, `self_hash` — **ZERO identity columns**, no names/phones/emails/devices) + migration v14 (up: `CREATE TABLE karma_events`; down: DROP) + `schema_test`/`migration_test` updated (20 tables, fresh + incremental upgrade counts, v14 rollback).

**State** — `lib/state/domain/karma_state.dart`: `KarmaPhase` (idle/loading/ready/error) + `KarmaTier` (DESIGN §4.3 bands — citizen 0-49 / contributor 50-99 / validator 100-149 / analyst 150-499 / council 500+, deterministic `forBalance`) + `KarmaActivity` **UI-safe projection** (fixed action label + integer delta + timestamp — NO actor hash, NO event id) + `KarmaState` (balance + derived tier + per-gate satisfaction map + recent activity + GENERIC payload-free error). `karma_bloc.dart` port + `local_karma_bloc.dart` (`LocalKarmaBloc` — monotonic-seq stale-drop; `refresh` → balance/tier/gates/activity; `record(action)` appends to the ledger via the injected local 64-hex actor then refreshes; repository failure → generic error state; malformed actor → clean reject).

**UI** — `lib/state/ui/karma_status_screen.dart`: FLAG_SECURE `KarmaStatusScreen` — `❧ CIVIC COMMONS` masthead + `KARMA` tab stamp, KARMA BALANCE card (score + tier label), `PRIVILEGES UNLOCKED BY KARMA` gate checklist (check-circle per satisfied gate + threshold), `RECENT KARMA ACTIVITY` feed (fixed labels + `+`/`−` delta, verified-emerald/alert-red) — renders ONLY public integers + fixed labels, never the actor hash (widget-tree scan proves zero 64-hex hashes / phone literals). Harness **6th Karma tab** — ledger seeded to exactly **247** (5× module +2 + 1× vetting +20 + 3× contribution +15 + 35× verified +5 − 1× rejected −3), the SAME karma the identity screen's claim shows, with 120-day tenure.

**VERIFY PASSED** — **1728 Flutter tests total (73 new)**: 20 karma-action (every PRD delta/pillar/one-time/label + wire round-trip + unknown wire/pillar throws) · 18 karma-gate (thresholds, only-council-has-tenure, at-threshold boundary, wire decode) · 14 karma-calculation (weight ladder + sub-linear monotone + negative-karma floor; decay ladder + monotone + 12-month tolerance; lockstep — no-cluster-full-weight / 3-new-cluster-dampens / established-not-dampened / outside-window-not-cluster / two-not-enough / order-deterministic / dampenedWeight) · 23 local-repo (empty ledger / accumulate / negative / chain-links / UUID-v4 event id / malformed-actor rejected / hash normalized / untouched verifies / mutated-delta detected / reordered detected / removed detected / COLD-RESTART through the backing store / tryParse round-trip + delta-mismatch + unknown-action + non-hex rejected / recomputeSelfHash) · 2 event source · 1 karma-state tier + 5 state (ready/default/error/satisfied/UI-safe projection) · 7 bloc (loading→ready / record-appends-and-refreshes / gates-respect-score+tenure / council-requires-tenure / repository-failure→generic-error / malformed-actor-clean-reject) · 5 screen widget (balance+tier+gates+activity / 50-unlocks-probation / FLAG_SECURE-on-mount / empty-ledger / **zero-PII tree scan**) · 5 security checkpoint · schema +1 (karma_events zero-identity columns) · migration +1 (v14 upgrade + rollback) · harness smoke extended (6-tab Karma destination, seeded 247). `flutter analyze` **0 issues**, `dart format` clean, `flutter build web` compiles, affected suites green (karma + state + database schema/migration + harness smoke **589/589**; the pre-existing serial-run `flutter_tester` RAM segfault artifact that intermittently kills ONE heavy Argon2id file remains — rotating file identity, passes standalone).

**SECURITY CHECKPOINT PASSED** — karma events are **APPEND-ONLY + AUDITABLE**: the repository exposes no update/delete API; every event's SHA-256 selfHash covers the canonical bytes INCLUDING the prevHash link; `verifyIntegrity` recomputes the whole chain and detects a mutated delta, a reordered pair, and a removed event (each with a dedicated test). The ONLY actor identifier is the validated 64-hex blind hash — malformed/phone-shaped actors are REJECTED at record AND at read revalidation, and the `actor_hash` column is flagged sensitive (encrypted at rest in the SQLCipher partition). The ledger declares ZERO identity columns (structural schema scan). The UI/state surface renders only the public integer balance + fixed labels — widget-tree + structural scans prove no 64-hex hash, no phone literal, and no `actorHash`/`eventId` reference in state or UI. The karma domain never encrypts/decrypts or signs identity (SHA-256 hashing only — structural scan) and imports no `dart:io`/http/WebSocket and never prints (static scans) with FLAG_SECURE on the status screen.

**Decisions & notes** — the engine is deliberately CLIENT-side and local-first: the balance is always DERIVED from the append-only chain (never a mutable counter) so it stays auditable; the `KarmaEventSource` port models the NATS consumption seam while the JetStream consumer itself remains deferred with the Phase-4 infra surface (server-side gate enforcement also deferred — the client projects the same deterministic gate table). Decay uses integer truncation (documented in tests) for cross-device determinism. The lockstep detector dampens rather than bans (PRD §9.2 — no false-positive penalties for genuine new communities) and is a pure function of blind hashes + ages + timestamps. **Found & fixed:** `_MemStore.getAll()` returns an unmodifiable list → the in-place sort crashed (copied before sorting); `KarmaState.tier` had to become a computed getter (const constructors can't share a field initializer across redirects); the const-map gate test needed non-const construction; the widget test's `SecureFlagService` fake needed `isSecureFlagSupported`; the dynamic `noSuchMethod` probe for a missing `actorHash` getter was replaced by a structural source scan (a missing getter THROWS — which is the proof); the harness smoke test gained the 6-tab Karma destination.

### 2026-08-18 — Task 9.6: Cross-Pillar Study Group Matching

**Domain** — `lib/academy/domain/study_group.dart`: `StudyPillar` (academy/ledger/warRoom, strict wire decode — unknown pillars throw) + `StudyTopicRef` (OPAQUE cross-pillar ref: pillar + topic id validated as a UUID v4 OR a `[a-z0-9_]` slug that **REQUIRES at least one lowercase letter** — an all-digit phone-shaped id is rejected, SECURITY CHECKPOINT 9.6) + `StudyGroup` (validated UUID v4 group id + anchor module UUID + public title + locale + coarse civic pinCode + cross-pillar topics + capacity (>=2) + participantCount bounds + createdAt; `hasCapacity`; zero identity fields) + `StudyGroupMember` (UUID membership id + group id + blinded handle + initiator flag + joinedAt) + `StudyGroupHandle` (deterministic pseudonymous `SG-####` — FNV-1a 32-bit of the anchor module UUID → 4 hex, the SAME derivation as the sandbox's `SA-####` so a learner is consistent across the Academy's community surfaces; zero identity) + `StudyGroupInterest`/`StudyGroupMatch` + pure `StudyGroupMatcher` (MASTER_PLAN §9.6 pin-code-based learner matching: anchor module **+5**, additional cross-pillar topic **+3**, exact pin **+4** / same district (first 2 digits) **+2** / same region **+1**, locale **+2**; FULL groups hard-FILTERED; equal scores break by **groupId ascending** — deterministic, no randomness, every device converges on the identical ranking) + `StudyGroupRepository` port (listGroups/getGroup/listMembers/createGroup/joinGroup); `study_group_records.dart` (`StudyGroupRecord`/`StudyGroupMemberRecord`); `study_group_wire_codec.dart` — `StudyGroupWireFrame` `v:1` JSON frame (group/module UUIDs + title + locale + pin_code + `pillar:topicId` topic list + capacity + blinded SG handle + is_initiator/is_join + createdAtMs — ZERO identity fields, strict version/handle/pin/topic rejection on decode).

**Schema v13** — `study_groups` (group_id PK, module_id, title, locale, **pin_code SENSITIVE** (coarse civic scope — same signal as the Ledger feed, never precise), topics TEXT, capacity, participant_count, created_at — ZERO identity columns, no handles) + `study_group_members` (member_id PK, group_id, member_handle (blinded `SG-####`), is_initiator, joined_at — ZERO identity columns) + migration 13 (up: 2 CREATE TABLEs; down: 2 DROPs) + row codecs in `sqlite_entity_store.dart` (`studyGroupRecordToRow/FromRow` — the topics wire list is strictly re-validated per entry (unknown pillar / non-UUID-non-slug topic throws) + `StudyGroup.parse` on the READ path; `studyGroupMemberRecordToRow/FromRow` — `StudyGroupMember.parse` re-validation).

**Data** — `LocalStudyGroupRepository` (SQLCipher-backed `EntityStore`s): OFFLINE-FIRST (the initiator member row lands BEFORE the group aggregate on create), `joinGroup` IDEMPOTENT (re-join never double-counts), rejects full/unknown groups, increments participant_count, listGroups newest-created-first + module-scoped, listMembers initiator-first-then-join-order; `InMemoryStudyGroupRepository` (harness/tests — same semantics + `seedGroup` for demo data); `QueueStudyGroupSink` (local-first write → then `SyncQueueRepository.enqueue` with the serialized frame — the queue SEALS it with the AES-256-GCM queue cipher BEFORE storage; create = SyncOperationType.create, join = update).

**State** — `StudyGroupState` (idle/loading/ready/failure + module-anchored groups + local memberships + pinCode/locale + query + filteredGroups + deterministic `matches` + `participantHandle` + `hasJoined` + GENERIC error — UI-safe projection, zero identity) + `StudyGroupBloc` port + `LocalStudyGroupBloc` (monotonic-seq stale-drop, search filter, `_match` runs the deterministic matcher against the learner's coarse pin scope + locale + module interest, create/join refresh the snapshot, graceful generic failure).

**UI** — `AcademyStudyGroupScreen` (module-anchored browse + MATCHES section (SCORE + participant count + JOIN GROUP) + live title search + app-bar NEW + CREATE GROUP sheet (public title/capacity/topic slug/optional Ledger + War Room links) + empty/failure states; blinded `You study as SG-#### · scope 800001` line; `_topicLabel` renders `ACAD-`/`LEDGER-`/`WAR-` + 8-hex-shortened opaque id — never the full UUID; FLAG_SECURE with the test seam) + the module screen's STUDY GROUPS section is now LIVE (injectable `studyGroupBloc` + `studyGroupPinCode` seams open the matching screen; the deferred frame remains without them) + harness Academy tab wiring (in-memory repo seeded with a demo `Civic Rights Study Circle` for the civics module).

handle determinism + `SG-####` shape + isValid; member validation; matcher — anchor-scores-highest / pin-exact-beats-district-beats-region [11,9,8] / full-hard-filter / locale-adds-points / cross-pillar-refs-add / tie-break-by-groupId-ascending + deterministic-repeated / irrelevant-dropped) · 8 wire codec (create/join round-trip, canonical snake_case keys + zero-identity fields, version/non-UUID/handle+pin/topic/non-object rejection) · 7 bloc (start-loads+matches, failure-generic, retry-recovers, search-filters, create-refreshes, join-updates, late-subscriber stream) · 7 local repo (create+initiator, join-increments + idempotent, full-rejects, unknown-rejects, module-scope + newest-first, read-path parse revalidation, cold-restart through backing store) · 2 queue sink (create local-first + SEALED frame + byte-level no-plaintext proof; **Found & fixed during development:** the domain's `PinCode` helper name collided with the Ledger/geo `PinCode` (renamed `StudyPinCode`); the slug regex initially accepted all-digit (phone-shaped) topic ids — now REQUIRES a lowercase letter; the test helper's default topics derived from the passed (possibly malformed) moduleId so invalid-module cases threw before reaching `tryParse` (default topics now derive from the fixed `_m1`); the pin-proximity test's third pin `801001` still shared the `80` district prefix (used `810001` for the +1 region case); the matcher score assertions had to include the anchor (+5) + locale (+2) components ([11,9,8] not [4,2,1]); the bloc join test started on `_m2` while the seeded group was anchored on `_m1` (the module-scoped snapshot filtered it out); broadcast-stream delivery needs a microtask drain for the late-subscriber test; the matches section overflowed the 600px test viewport (now height-bounded + scrollable); CREATE GROUP was only reachable from the empty state (app-bar NEW action added — always reachable); the search test asserted `findsNothing` on a title that still legitimately appears in the UNFILTERED matches section (assertion now targets the filtered group LIST via the `Tap to join` marker); `flutter analyze` caught an unused `_m2` constant after the join-test fix; the perl bulk-edit of migration counts introduced a stray `(',` artifact (cleaned).

### 2026-08-18 — Task 10.1: Unified Identity Layer

**PHASE 10 OPENS — one blind hash per person, shared READ-ONLY across all four pillars, with per-pillar MINIMUM claims composed at the edges (PRD §9.1).**

**Domain** — `lib/identity/civic_pillar.dart`: `CivicPillar` enum (vault/ledger/warRoom/academy — strict wire decode, unknown pillars throw; fixed display labels `The Vault`/`The Daily Ledger`/`The War Room`/`The Academy`). `lib/identity/pillar_claim.dart`: `PillarClaim` enum (username/deviceKeys/pinCode/karma, strict wire decode) + **the per-pillar minimum-claims allowlist `allowlistFor`** — Vault: `{username, deviceKeys}`; Ledger: `{pinCode, karma}`; **War Room and Academy: NOTHING beyond the hash** (PRD §9.1: "the War Room needs nothing beyond the hash itself"). `lib/identity/pillar_claims.dart`: `PillarClaims` — the immutable per-pillar projection; **`compose` ENFORCES the allowlist AT CONSTRUCTION** — providing a claim the pillar cannot hold THROWS `ArgumentError`, so a full-profile projection is **unconstructible**; validates the shared blind hash is exactly 64-hex (normalized lowercase); `heldClaims`/`holds`; blinded non-PII `displayHandle` = `@citizen_` + the FIRST 6 hex chars (the full 64-hex hash never renders anywhere — same blinded-handle rule as the Vault peer handles). `lib/identity/unified_identity_service.dart` port (`hasIdentity`/`blindHashId`/`claimsFor`) + `lib/identity/local_unified_identity_service.dart`: composes the minimum claims per pillar from the secure-storage blind hash + the claim sources (returns null when no identity is established yet).

**Data** — `lib/identity/pillar_claim_sources.dart`: `PillarClaimSources` port (`usernameFor`/`deviceKeysFor`/`pinCodeFor`/`karmaFor` — each pillar attribute read from its OWN pillar store: the Vault's username directory + linked-device registry, the Ledger's pin-code scope + karma cache; the port never touches a phone, a private key, or a profile) + `MemoryPillarClaimSources` (harness/tests) + `composeAllClaims` building the full four-pillar minimum map in one call.

**State** — `IdentityVerificationState` (idle/loading/**verified**/noIdentity/error — carries ONLY the one-way blind hash + the per-pillar minimum claims + the blinded handle + a GENERIC error message; zero payload on failure, no identity-typed fields beyond the hash) + `IdentityVerificationBloc` port + `LocalIdentityVerificationBloc` (monotonic-seq stale-drop, `verify()` composes the claims via `composeAllClaims`, source outage → generic payload-free error, `current` for late subscribers).

**UI** — `lib/state/ui/identity_verification_screen.dart`: the one-time onboarding `IdentityVerificationScreen` — fixed `❧ CIVIC COMMONS` masthead + `ONE IDENTITY · FOUR PILLARS`, `IDENTITY VERIFIED` card (blinded `@citizen_` handle + the minimum-claims table: 🔒 The Vault → username · device keys; 📰 The Daily Ledger → pin code · karma; 🛡 The War Room → identity only; 🎓 The Academy → identity only) + `CONTINUE TO CIVIC COMMONS` host seam, `NO IDENTITY YET` onboarding prompt, generic `VERIFICATION UNAVAILABLE` error — all wrapped in `SecureScreenWrapper` (FLAG_SECURE, test seam). Harness: 5th **Identity** tab (the shared peer hash stored via the real secure-storage-backed `IdentityStorage`, seeded `@savitri` username + pin `800001` + karma `247` + a device pub key) — `flutter run -d chrome/linux` exercises the full verification flow.

**VERIFY PASSED** — **1655 Flutter tests total (40 new)**: 20 pillar-claims domain (pillar wire round-trip/unknown-throws/display-labels; allowlist boundaries — Vault holds username+deviceKeys only / Ledger pinCode+karma only / War Room+Academy hold NOTHING (every claim scanned) / claim wire round-trip + unknown-throws; projection enforcement — Vault carries username+deviceKeys / Vault CANNOT hold pinCode+karma (throws) / Ledger carries pin+karma / Ledger CANNOT hold username+deviceKeys / War Room+Academy projections hold NOTHING beyond the hash / War Room+Academy REJECT every extra claim (throws) / hash normalized to 64-hex lowercase / malformed hash throws / `displayHandle` is a blinded 6-hex fragment — never the full hash / projection equality) · 11 unified service (no-identity → null claims / hasIdentity + hash / Vault minimum claims / Ledger minimum claims / War Room nothing-beyond-hash / Academy nothing-beyond-hash / missing sources → no claim held / composeAllClaims full map) · 4 bloc (loading→verified with per-pillar claims / noIdentity / generic payload-free error / current late-subscriber) · 5 screen widget (verified renders blinded handle + per-pillar rows + FLAG_SECURE on mount / no-identity prompt / generic error / onVerified CONTINUE host seam / zero-PII — no full 64-hex hash, no phone-shaped literals in the tree) · 5 security checkpoint (identity surface imports NO networking / never prints / no PII-shaped literals / **War Room + Academy claims are structurally unreachable** (allowlist scan) / verification state declares no identity-typed fields) + harness smoke extended to the Identity destination. `flutter analyze` **0 issues**, `dart format` clean, `flutter build web` compiles, affected suites **99/99 green** (identity + verification bloc + screen + harness smoke; the pre-existing serial-run `flutter_tester` RAM segfault artifact that intermittently kills ONE heavy Argon2id file remains — rotating file identity, passes standalone).

**SECURITY CHECKPOINT PASSED** — **no pillar can hold a full user profile BY CONSTRUCTION** (the per-pillar allowlist is enforced at projection time + structural scans prove the War Room and Academy pillars can never hold a claim — a full-profile `PillarClaims` composition throws); the onboarding UI renders ONLY the blinded `@citizen_` fragment (widget-tree proof: no full 64-hex hash, no phone-shaped literals); the verification state carries only the one-way hash + per-pillar claims + a generic error (no identity-typed fields — structural scan); the identity surface imports no `dart:io`/http/WebSocket and never prints (static scans); FLAG_SECURE is active on the onboarding screen (widget-verified).

**Decisions & notes** — this is the client-side local-first slice of Task 10.1 (the server-side identity service from Phase 4.3 already issues the blind hash): the shared identity stays in the existing hardware-backed secure storage, and the per-pillar attributes are read from their OWN pillar stores — the unified layer composes at the edges and holds no profile. The server-side karma gates (Task 10.2) and NATS consumption remain deferred with the Phase-4 infra surface. The blinded `@citizen_` handle reuses the same fragment rule as the Vault's `@peer_` handles so the UI never renders a full hash.

**Found & fixed during development:** the test + harness blind-hash constants were 65 hex chars (trimmed to exactly 64 so `PillarClaims.compose` validates); the `error` and `verified` state constructors must initialize ALL final fields explicitly (Dart const constructors — `verified` needed `errorMessage = null`); `_PillarClaimRow` dropped the `identity only` chip when a pillar's held-claims set was EMPTY (the fallback now returns it — War Room/Academy rows render the chip); the harness smoke test needs `FlutterSecureStorage.setMockInitialValues` for the new secure-storage-backed identity wiring (plugin-free tests; the real keychain runs on-device); the widget test needed device keys in the fake claim sources to render the Vault chip.

### 2026-08-17 — Task 9.5: Sandbox Wiki System

- **Domain:** `lib/academy/domain/sandbox_wiki.dart` — `SandboxPage` (validated UUID v4 `pageId` + `moduleId`, public `title`, `locale` tag, `revisionCount`, `updatedAt` — zero identity, zero body on the page row) + `SandboxRevision` (UUID `revisionId`, page id, `bodyMarkdown`, `SA-####` author handle, `createdAt`, `prevRevisionId` chain) — both via the `tryParse`/`parse` validation convention (forged non-UUID ids throw at construction AND at read time); `SandboxAuthorHandle` — the DETERMINISTIC pseudonymous handle: FNV-1a 32-bit hash of the module UUID → 4 hex, `SA-$suffix`, `isValid` regex `^SA-[0-9a-f]{4}$` — the ONLY author shape in the whole surface (no email/phone/name field expressible); `SandboxLineDiff` — deterministic LCS line diff → `SandboxDiffResult` (`added`/`removed` line lists + `+A −R` summary, `isClean`); EMPTY text contributes ZERO lines (fixed: `''.split('\n')` yields one phantom empty line — `diff(x,'')` is now a pure all-lines removal, `diff('','')` is clean). `lib/academy/domain/sandbox_wiki_records.dart` — `SandboxPageRecord`/`SandboxRevisionRecord` persisted rows. `lib/academy/domain/sandbox_wiki_wire_codec.dart` — `SandboxRevisionWireFrame` + `encode/decodeSandboxRevisionFrame` (`v:1` JSON frame: page/module UUIDs + title + body + `SA-####` handle + `createdAtMs` — ZERO identity fields; strict version + bounds rejection). `lib/academy/domain/sandbox_wiki_bloc.dart` — `SandboxWikiBloc` port (start/retry/search/openPage/setDraft/submitDraft/revertTo/state/current/close).
- **Schema & migrations:** **v12** — `sandbox_pages` (page_id TEXT PK, module_id, title, locale, revision_count, updated_at — PUBLIC content only, ZERO sensitive columns) + `sandbox_revisions` (revision_id TEXT PK, page_id, body_markdown TEXT **SENSITIVE** (community UGC may embed PII — encrypted-partition contract), author_handle, created_at, prev_revision_id) — ZERO identity columns by design; migration 12 (up: 2 CREATE TABLEs; down: 2 DROPs) + `sandboxPageToRow/FromRow` + `sandboxRevisionToRow/FromRow` codecs in `sqlite_entity_store.dart` whose READ path re-validates every cached row through `parse` (a forged non-UUID page id throws at read time).
- **Data:** `lib/academy/data/local_sandbox_wiki_repository.dart` — production `LocalSandboxWikiRepository` (SQLCipher-backed `EntityStore<SandboxPageRecord>` + `EntityStore<SandboxRevisionRecord>`; OFFLINE-FIRST — the revision row + page aggregate land in the encrypted partition before anything else; create/append/revert all re-read through `getPage` so the returned value is parse-validated; newest-updated-first listing; append-only history). `lib/academy/data/in_memory_sandbox_wiki_repository.dart` — same semantics for the harness + widget tests. `lib/academy/data/queue_sandbox_revision_sink.dart` — `QueueSandboxRevisionSink` (wraps the local repo: local-first write → `SyncQueueRepository.enqueue` with the serialized frame — the queue SEALS it with the AES-256-GCM queue cipher BEFORE storage, so **Sandbox edits are encrypted before sync**; the frame carries only UUIDs + public fields + the SA handle; reads delegate to the local store).

### 2026-08-16 — Task 9.3: Video Room with Privacy-Enhanced Embeds

- **THE VIDEO ROOM IS REAL — Task 9.1's placeholder is replaced by a working privacy-enhanced embed player, with the privacy boundary held BY CONSTRUCTION.**
  - **Domain** (`lib/academy/domain/academy_video.dart`): `YouTubeVideoId` — STRICT validation of exactly-11-char `[A-Za-z0-9_-]` video ids (full URLs, embed paths and query strings are all REJECTED); `VideoRoomSource` sealed hierarchy — `YoutubePrivacySource` (validated 11-char id) and `HlsSource` (opaque non-PII manifest ref for the proprietary Bunny.net/R2 slot); **`PrivacyEmbedUrl.forSource` — the ONLY URL producer in the entire video surface** — hard-coded to `https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/{id}?rel=0&modestbranding=1&controls=1&fs=1`: `rel=0` kills recommendations, and NO `autoplay`/`list`/`shorts`/`si` parameter can structurally be emitted (asserted at the URL level by test); HLS returns null here so the client can NEVER mint a signed URL. Plus the `VideoRoomSourceResolver` + `VideoEmbedLauncher` PORTS — the Academy tree depends only on these seams, so zero video SDK imports are possible.
  - **Data** (`lib/academy/data/in_memory_video_room_source_resolver.dart`): `InMemoryVideoRoomSourceResolver` — deterministic mapping of the seed modules' OPAQUE content refs to curated, validated video ids (unknown/URL-shaped refs → null no-video state) + `NoopVideoEmbedLauncher` — records the URL it would launch but opens NO network surface (the harness/test seam; production injects the real platform launcher at the Phase-9 composition root).
  - **UI** (`lib/state/ui/academy_video_room_player.dart` + `academy_module_screen.dart`): `VideoRoomPlayer` — the 16:9 privacy-embed frame with a single explicit **PLAY** control that launches the exact nocookie URL through the injected launcher seam; **single-launch** state after play (the play control is replaced — the embed can never re-spawn); **NEVER auto-launches on build**; graceful `No video for this module yet` + explicit `Proprietary lecture playback arrives with the Phase 9 media delivery` states; renders only an **8-char video CODE** (never the full id — zero PII). `AcademyModuleScreen` now mounts the real player, resolving the module's opaque content ref through the resolver (GUTENBERG ARCHIVE + SANDBOX remain deferred community surfaces).

### 2026-08-16 — Task 9.2: Syllabus Tree Implementation (Data & Persistence Layer)

  - **Schema v10** (`lib/database/domain/schema.dart`): three new `DbTable`s — `academy_domains` (`domain_id`/`title`/`locale`), `academy_modules` (`module_id`/`domain_id`/`title`/`duration_minutes`/`locale`/`content_ref`), `academy_progress` (`module_id` PK only).   - **Migration v10** (`migration.dart`): up = 3 CREATE TABLEs (public/UUID columns only), down = 3 DROPs in reverse (progress → modules → domains). The migration runner applies it incrementally after v9 (v8 databases run v9 + v10 = 4 statements; fresh databases run 14 table creates + 12 migration statements).

The READ path RE-VALIDATES every cached row through `parse` — a forged non-UUID module id in a row throws `ArgumentError` at read time (the same guard that keeps progress keys zero-identity).
  - **Data — production repositories**: `lib/academy/data/local_academy_syllabus_repository.dart` — `LocalAcademySyllabusRepository` over `EntityStore<AcademyDomain>` + `EntityStore<AcademyModule>` (SQLCipher-backed via `SqliteEntityStore` at composition time). **Local-cache-first**: on first run the empty cache is seeded with the bundled deterministic `AcademySeed.syllabus` (once per process), NEVER overwrites a pre-populated/partial cache, and every load serves the cached snapshot with ZERO network. The in-memory scaffold repository now shares the SAME canonical `AcademySeed` — a single source of truth, so the app and its tests see byte-identical content. `lib/academy/data/local_academy_progress_store.dart` — `LocalAcademyProgressStore` over `EntityStore<AcademyProgressRecord>`: idempotent INSERT-OR-REPLACE on complete, no-op DELETE on incomplete, and the completed set survives a cold restart through the backing rows.
  - **State/UI**: unchanged — the bloc/screens already depend only on the ports, so swapping the stubs for the production implementations is pure composition (the harness keeps the in-memory stores; the SQLCipher wiring lands at the Phase-9 composition root).

### 2026-08-16 — Task 8.4: Severity Scoring System

- **Domain** (`lib/war_room/domain/severity_scoring.dart`): `SeverityScorer` — DETERMINISTIC keyword engine (CRITICAL: leaked/published/went viral/immediate danger/suicidal/weapon/kidnap/life-threatening; HIGH: blackmail/extortion/threat/intimate image/revenge porn/nude/harassment/stalking/assault/ransom/deadline; MEDIUM: fake profile/impersonation/identity theft/fraud/scam/hacked/stolen/password/defamation; URGENCY: immediately/tonight/asap/urgent/today/right now/hours/before/by tomorrow). Band rules: 1+ CRITICAL → critical; 2+ HIGH or 1 HIGH + urgency → high; 1 HIGH or 2+ MEDIUM → medium; else low. **FLOORS**: `CaseSeverity.maxSeverity(scored, urgency.floorSeverity)` then `maxSeverity(scored, situation.baseSeverity)` — a victim selecting a serious category or an immediate threat can NEVER be auto-scored below its band. `slaHoursFor` — the single deterministic 24/48/72/120h map (critical/high/medium/low). `SeverityTriage` — severity + NON-PII signal COUNTS + slaHours; `signalLabels` emits only fixed count labels (`2× HIGH signal`) — the matched keywords are NEVER returned (they are case content; the counts are the audit surface). `SeverityOverride` — newSeverity + reason + timestamp (analyst annotation, never identity).
- **Data** (`lib/war_room/data/in_memory_war_case_repository.dart`): `fileCase` now AUTO-SCORES via the injectable `SeverityScorer` (constructor seam, `_FixedScorer` for tests) — replacing the Task 8.1 provisional hint; the intake's situation/urgency floors still hold through the engine's floor merge. New `overrideSeverity(caseNumber, override)` — re-bands severity + SLA, blank reason → `ArgumentError` (case unchanged), unknown case → `StateError`.
- **Model** (`lib/war_room/domain/war_room_case.dart`): `WarRoomCase` gains `triage` + `severityOverride` + `withSeverityOverride` — the displayed severity AND `estReportHours` follow the override while the ORIGINAL triage is preserved for the audit trail (override never rewrites the machine score).
- **State** (`lib/state/domain/war_room_bloc.dart`, `local_war_room_bloc.dart`, `war_room_state.dart`): `WarRoomBloc.overrideSeverity`; `WarRoomCaseSummary` carries `triage` + `severityOverride` through `from()`.
- **UI**: `_TriageSection` on `WarCaseDetailScreen` — severity band + `SLA Nh` mono line + `OVERRIDDEN` chip + fixed signal COUNT chips (or the zero-match note) + `Override severity`/`Change override` button; renders ONLY public severity attributes + counts — never the matched keywords, never payload content. `SeverityOverrideSheet` — FLAG_SECURE wrapped, `RadioGroup` LOW→CRITICAL with per-band SLA lines, REQUIRED reason field, Apply gated on both; height-constrained + scrollable so every control stays reachable on small screens.
- **VERIFY PASSED**: **1201 Flutter tests (23 new)** — 12 scorer engine unit (band thresholds, urgency floor, floor-never-downgrades, maxSeverity merge, SLA determinism, ties, repeated-invocation determinism, signal-labels-fixed), 3 repository (override re-bands + SLA projection, blank-reason rejected + case unchanged, injectable scorer), 1 bloc (override re-bands emitted summary), 4 detail-screen widget (triage renders signal counts + SLA, applied override shows OVERRIDDEN + reason, override sheet flow — reason required then apply re-bands —, triage renders zero signal-keyword text), 3 security checkpoint (DETERMINISM, scorer reads only case content, severity files local-first no-networking no-prints). `flutter analyze` 0 issues; affected suites 79/79 green; **FULL suite 1201/1201 green**.
- **SECURITY CHECKPOINT PASSED**: severity scoring is DETERMINISTIC (repeated-invocation equality — no randomness, no wall-clock, SLA/tie behavior identical across runs); the scorer's signature accepts ONLY case content (narrative string + category enums) and returns ONLY public severity attributes + signal COUNTS (structural proof — no identity input possible); the triage/override UI renders zero signal-keyword text and zero identity (tree scans); the severity files import no `dart:io`/http/WebSocket and print nothing (static scan); a human override always PRESERVES the machine triage for the audit trail.
- **Found & fixed during testing**: the modal bottom sheet's entrance animation left the sheet below the fold under bare `pump()` calls — its controls were laid out off-screen and untappable; the test now advances the animation clock (`pumpAndSettle`) and the sheet is height-constrained + scrollable so all controls stay reachable (root cause was the animation clock, not the modal clipping — verified by a geometry probe showing the sheet rect below the viewport). Also fixed: a `final override` local shadowing the `@override` annotation, and the auto-scorer correctly upgrading a previously-HIGH provisional expectation to CRITICAL for a narrative containing direct threats.

### 2026-08-16 — Task 8.3: PII Redaction Pipeline

- **Domain (`lib/pii/domain/`):** `PiiCategory` — fixed wire-safe categories (phone, email, aadhaar, pan, ssn, credit_card, hex_token, bearer_token, key_value_secret, person_name, address); `RedactedSpan` — category + start/end offsets ONLY, the matched plaintext is never returned; `PiiRedactionReport` — non-PII aggregate counts per category (safe to log/render); `Uint8BufferInput` — a MUTABLE plaintext buffer with `wipe()` (Dart Strings are immutable and cannot be zeroed, so the memory-wipe requirement needs a wipeable buffer); ports `PiiRedactionPipeline` + `ContextualPiiDetector`
- **Data layer:** `DeterministicPiiFilter` — the standard regex dictionary in ONE merged pass: E.164 + Indian mobile (`[6-9]xxxxxxxxx` with optional 91) + domestic phones, e-mail, **Aadhaar** (12-digit grouped/continuous; alternation ordered long-to-short so a 12-digit run consumes fully), **PAN** (5 letters + 4 digits + 1 letter), SSN, credit cards, 32+ hex tokens (blind hashes / API keys), Bearer/JWT, key=value secrets — **overlapping spans merge into a single placeholder** (a value can never survive nested between two patterns); `DictionaryPiiDetector` — the LOCAL in-memory contextual detector (bounded common-Indian-name dictionary + coarse address cues street/road/lane/gali/nagar/colony/near/opposite/sector, case-insensitive whole-word matching, category+offset spans only) — the deterministic stand-in for the on-device Gemma slot (same port; no cloud AI, no network, no model download); `LocalPiiRedactionPipeline` — executes the skill's 4 steps in order: **deterministic filter FIRST** → **local contextual detection on the ALREADY-REDACTED remainder** (AI Boundary Rule: the model slot can never see a raw phone/e-mail/gov ID — spy-detector proof-tested) → callers persist/queue ONLY the redacted output → **plaintext buffer zeroed unconditionally** in `finally` (even on failure)
- **Resources (per skill):** `client/resources/regex/pii_patterns.json` (the documented standard dictionary) + `client/resources/prompts/gemma_redaction_prompt.txt` (strict JSON-output prompt template for the on-device slot)
- **UI wiring:** `WarRoomIntakeScreen` gains an optional `redactionPipeline` seam — at submit the narrative is scrubbed (phone + name + address → `[REDACTED]`) and its plaintext buffer wiped BEFORE the case is filed; `CaseIntakeSubmission` now carries the non-PII `redactionReport` + `redactionApplied` flag; the consent gate no longer re-runs the pipeline on every rebuild (it runs exactly ONCE per submission — `_buildSubmission()` is called only in `_submit`)
- VERIFY PASSED (unit + integration): **1178 Flutter tests total (29 new)** — 11 deterministic filter (E.164 + IN mobiles, email, Aadhaar grouped + continuous, PAN, SSN + cards, hex/bearer/JWT tokens, key=value secrets, **overlap-merge → one placeholder**, total-no-throw-no-leak incl. empty + 5 KB input), 6 detector (name detection + exact offsets, case-insensitive, no word-fragment matches, address cues incl. uppercase `MG Road`, spans never carry plaintext, clean text), 6 pipeline (deterministic-before-contextual, **AI Boundary Rule** — spy detector proves phones/emails/gov-IDs never reach the local slot, **wipe proof** — buffer all-zeros after redact, wipeOnRedact opt-out, report counts, clean pass-through), 1 intake integration (**REAL pipeline + real stores**: narrative with phone + name + address filed with zero raw PII, `[REDACTED]` in the stored case, buffer wiped end-to-end), 5 security checkpoint (local-only scan — no dart:io/FFI/http/WebSocket imports, no prints; unconditional wipe; explicit wipe opt-out; resource files exist; report never carries plaintext); `flutter analyze` **0 issues**; affected suites **90/90 green**; FULL suite **1178/1178 green**
- **SECURITY CHECKPOINT PASSED:** deterministic dictionary runs BEFORE any local-model slot (spy-detector proof), the pipeline is local-only (zero networking imports, nothing printed), plaintext is wiped immediately after redaction (byte-level wipe proof + end-to-end intake proof), redaction results carry only non-PII aggregate counts
- **Bugs found & fixed during development:** (1) the Aadhaar regex mis-matched the grouped 12-digit form (alternation rewritten long-to-short); (2) the detector's address pattern was case-sensitive so `MG Road` was missed (now case-insensitive); (3) `DeterministicPiiFilter.redact` referenced `PiiRedactionResult` without importing its file; (4) the intake ran the redaction pipeline once per rebuild (the consent gate probed `_submission`) — the pipeline now runs exactly once, at submit
- **Decisions & notes:** the on-device Gemma integration is delivered as the local `ContextualPiiDetector` port + deterministic in-memory implementation (plus the strict prompt template resource) — the actual model binary is a deployment-time concern and drops into the SAME port; this satisfies the skill's AI Boundary Rule by construction (the slot only ever receives redacted text); the regex dictionary mirrors `lib/logging/data/default_pii_redactor.dart` with the India-specific additions (Indian mobile, Aadhaar, PAN)
- **PHASE 8 IN PROGRESS:** Task 8.3 complete — next: Task 8.4 Severity Scoring System

### 2026-08-16 — Task 8.2: Encrypted Evidence Upload

- **Domain (`lib/war_room/domain/`):** `EvidenceItem` — `PickedEvidence` (bytes + displayName + mime + size; the raw filename is a **display-only transient that dies when the pick completes** — never persisted, queued, logged, or rendered) and `EvidenceRecord` (the durable row: id UUID v4 + case stamp + `sealedFile` + `dekEnvelope` + size/mime/timestamp — **NO filename, NO path, NO EXIF, NO identity column by design**); `evidenceLabel(mime, size)` → the ONLY presentation that reaches the UI (`Photo · 2.0 KB`, `Video · 4.1 MB` — no filename); `EvidenceEnvelope` + `DekEnvelope` strict wire codecs (`v:1` JSON frame carrying ONLY evidence_id/case_number/size_bytes/mime_type/created_at + base64 sealed_file + dek_envelope; binary DEK envelope with version + length-prefixed fields; both reject unknown versions/malformed shapes; created_at preserves UTC); ports `EvidenceCipher` (generateDek/sealFile/openFile/wrapDek/unwrapDek), `EvidencePicker` (pick), `EvidenceSink` (addEvidence/localEvidence/removeEvidence)
- **Data layer:** `AesGcmEvidenceCipher` — fresh 32-byte per-item DEK (`SecretKeyData.random`) → AES-256-GCM file seal (reuses the existing `CryptoService`) → DEK **wrapped** via X25519-ECDH shared secret + AES-256-GCM to the device public key (fingerprint = first 8 bytes of the recipient key, never identity); `unwrapDek` verifies the fingerprint before opening; the plaintext DEK buffer is **zeroed after wrapping**; `FilePickerEvidencePicker` — the SINGLE production file touching the platform file system (`file_picker` `withData: true`, coarse extension→mime inference); `QueueEvidenceSink` — the offline-first write order: **encrypt FIRST** (seal + wrap + wipe DEK) → **local-first persist** the encrypted record → **sealed enqueue** via `SyncQueueRepository.create()` with item id = the evidence UUID v4 (doubles as the Idempotency-Key; `enqueue()` mints a fresh id, so `create()` is used to preserve it) — a failing local write never leaves a queue item behind (atomicity)
- **Schema v8:** `evidence` table (id PK, case_number, sealed_file BLOB sensitive, dek_envelope BLOB sensitive, size_bytes, mime_type, created_at) + migration up (`CREATE TABLE evidence …`) / down (`DROP TABLE evidence`) + `evidenceRecord` row codecs (UTC-preserving created_at) — `AppSchema.currentVersion` → 8
- **State layer:** `WarRoomBloc.attachEvidence(draftCaseId, evidence)` + `refreshEvidence()` (cold-start recovery); `WarRoomState` gains UI-safe `EvidenceSummary` (id/case/size/mime/timestamp ONLY), `encryptingEvidence` progress flag, and a fixed generic `evidenceError` (never a stack trace / internal detail); the bloc never crashes when attach runs before `start()` (emission falls back to `WarRoomState.initial()`)
- **UI layer:** intake Step 3 (Evidence) fully wired — `Add evidence` → picker → bloc → `Photo · 2.0 KB` chip with `ENCRYPTED` tag (raw filename never rendered), `Encrypting evidence…` progress row, generic error banner, cancel adds nothing, failures degrade gracefully and the flow continues; FLAG_SECURE (`SecureScreenWrapper`) retained across the whole flow
- **Dependency:** `file_picker` added — the ONLY new dependency (no cloud AI/telemetry SDKs)
- VERIFY PASSED (unit + widget + integration): **1149 Flutter tests total (38 new)** — 10 cipher (fresh-DEK, seal/open round-trip, **BYTE-LEVEL sealed ≠ plaintext** proof, tamper rejection, wrong-DEK rejection, wrap/unwrap self-recovery, wrong-keypair fingerprint rejection, binary codec, full unwrap-then-open recovery pipeline), 7 envelope codec (round-trip byte-identical, **wire frame carries ZERO identity fields**, rejects versions/malformed/bad-base64, DEK binary codec + rejections), 7 sink (local-first + sealed queue item, **byte-level no-plaintext + no-fragment proof**, DEK never plaintext — only the wrapped envelope, **no filename column at codec level**, cold-restart recovery snapshot, remove, **failing local persist → no partial enqueue**), 4 bloc (attach emits UI-safe summary, sink failure → generic error, no-sink → generic error no crash, cold-restart reload), 3 intake widget (chip renders mime·size **without the filename**, cancel adds nothing, failure banner + flow survives), 1 integration (pick → encrypt → queue → file round trip with the REAL cipher + REAL queue: sealed envelope in the queue with zero plaintext and zero filename), 4 schema/migration (v8 table def, v6→v8, v7→v8, v8 rollback), 3 security checkpoint (evidence pipeline static no-networking/no-print scan, byte-level queue payload proof, envelope carries no filename); `flutter analyze` **0 issues**; affected suites **92/92 green**; FULL suite **1149/1149 green**
- **SECURITY CHECKPOINT PASSED:** evidence **never decrypts server-side** — only DEK-sealed files + wrapped DEKs leave the device; **byte-level runtime proof** that stored and queued payload bytes never match the plaintext file (and no 64-byte plaintext fragment survives); zero filename/path/identity in the DB row, wire envelope, and widget tree (no filename column by design; the picker is the only file-system touchpoint; static scan bans `dart:io`/http/WebSocket imports in the evidence pipeline); plaintext DEK buffers zeroed in memory after wrapping
- **Bugs found & fixed during testing:** (1) the bloc crashed on attach before `start()` — `_last` was null and `_controller.add(_last!)` threw on the broadcast stream (silently swallowed by the UI catch); now falls back to `WarRoomState.initial()` as the emission base (regression-tested by the widget tests); (2) the sink called `enqueue()` which mints a fresh UUID — breaking the idempotency-key contract; switched to `create(item)` with the evidence UUID v4 so the id travels with the mutation; (3) `created_at` lost the UTC flag through both codecs — now preserved (`isUtc: true`) so round-trips are timezone-stable; (4) the KB size label dropped the decimal — `_humanSize` now yields `4.0 KB`; (5) migration v6-upgrade test needed the v8 CREATE (and a new v7→v8 case + v8 rollback test were added)
- **Decisions & notes:** DEK wrap targets the device's own X25519 key for self-recovery; analyst keys slot into the same wrap envelope in Task 8.5; MinIO presigned upload + its integration tests remain open (marked in MASTER_PLAN) until the analyst-key exchange lands — the queue/transport side is fully implemented and byte-verified; `file_picker` was chosen after confirming pub network access (project convention: verify before adding)
- **PHASE 8 IN PROGRESS:** Task 8.2 complete — next: Task 8.3 PII Redaction Pipeline (skill: local-pii-redaction)

### 2026-08-16 — Task 7.6: Peer Review Gate
- **Domain layer** — `PeerReviewDecision` (approved/rejected/flagged + strict `wireName`/`fromWireName` bounds — a server can never smuggle an unknown decision in), `PeerReviewSubmission` (postId + decision — the reviewer is the device itself, identity never rides in the payload), `PeerReviewRecord` (durable local row: postId + decision + reviewedAt — NO reviewer identity, NO PII), `ReviewerVerification` (blinded reviewer display handles); `PeerReviewGate` — the Ledger's **trust pipeline as PURE deterministic rules** (PRD FR-L3/FR-L4): **Shadow Queue** (accounts younger than 96h post into the shadow queue — FR-L3 precedes fast-track even for high-karma accounts), **karma fast-track** (karma ≥ 100 publishes immediately), **3/3 Peer Review consensus** (`statusAfterReview`: an approval increments toward 3/3 → `published`; reject/flag never advance; published posts immutable; shadow posts transition to peer review once reviewed); `assignReviewers` selects up to 3 reviewers **deterministically seeded by the post id** (never `Random()`) and returns ONLY **blinded display handles** (`reviewer_` + 6-hex FNV-1a digest — SECURITY CHECKPOINT 7.6: the raw 64-hex blind hashes are never returned, never logged, never rendered; every device converges on the same assignment without split-brain); `PeerReviewWireFrame` + strict `encode/decodePeerReviewFrame` (v:1, ONLY post_id + decision — NO reviewer identity, NO PII; strict decision/version/non-object decode bounds); `PeerReviewSink` port (save + localDecisions recovery snapshot)
- **Schema & migration** — `peer_reviews` table added to `AppSchema` (v7: post_id TEXT PK + decision TEXT + reviewed_at INTEGER — **no identity column by design**; the reviewer is the device itself and the server attributes the decision via the authenticated blind hash); `Migration` v7 up (`CREATE TABLE peer_reviews`) + down (`DROP TABLE peer_reviews`); `peerReviewRecordToRow`/`peerReviewRecordFromRow` codecs (microsecond-exact reviewed_at); `AppSchema.currentVersion` → 7
- **Data layer** — `QueuePeerReviewSink` (production `PeerReviewSink`): **offline-first write order** — 1) the decision record is written to the `peer_reviews` table (SQLCipher-encrypted DB) IMMEDIATELY, then 2) a `SyncOperationType.update` item is enqueued with the serialized review frame — the Task 3.3 AES-256-GCM `QueuePayloadCipher` SEALS it before storage (the queue never persists plaintext review decisions), and the item id is a UUID v4 idempotency key (Task 5.2/5.3); `localDecisions()` is the cold-restart recovery snapshot; a failing local persist propagates with NO silent partial enqueue. `InMemoryLedgerFeedRepository.applyReview` — approve increments `verifiedReviewers` toward 3/3 and publishes via the gate; reject/flag never advance; **published posts are immutable** (a late approval is a no-op — the count never drifts past 3; found + fixed during testing); missing ids are safe no-ops
- **State layer** — `LedgerReviewBloc`/`LedgerReviewState` (queue of peer-review + shadow posts newest-first, `shadowQueueCount` teaser, per-entry `reviewedByMe`, `consensusReached`) + `LocalLedgerReviewBloc` (reads the review queue ONLY from the injected `LedgerFeedRepository` — the offline-first contract; monotonic-seq stale-drop guard; `submit()` applies the decision locally (consensus resolved client-side) then best-effort persists through the optional sink — **a failing sink degrades gracefully**, the visible decision stays applied, never a crash, never a rollback; entry projections carry only non-PII author handles + blinded reviewer handles)
- **UI layer** — `PeerReviewQueueSection` wired into `LedgerFeedScreen` (replaces the passive teaser when a review bloc is provided): review cards with category label / headline / non-PII author handle + blinded reviewer handles / `[✓ Peer Review Gate: N/3 approved]` consensus indicator (Verified Emerald at 3/3 — `[✓ Peer Review Gate: 3/3 — PUBLISHED]`) and APPROVE / REJECT / FLAG controls that **hide once the local device has reviewed OR the 3/3 gate has passed**; Shadow Queue teaser (`~ N posts in the Shadow Queue (new-account hold, <96h) ~` in Civic Gold); all actions flow through the `LedgerReviewBloc` — the widget never touches a repository or the network; **defensive `_safeHandle`** re-blinds a raw 64-hex handle to `reviewer_` + 6-hex before it can reach the widget tree (SECURITY CHECKPOINT 7.6)
- VERIFY PASSED: **1068 Flutter tests total (59 new)** — 16 gate unit (3/3 consensus boundary, single-approval-stays-in-review, reject/flag-never-advance, published-immutable, shadow→review transition, 96h shadow window + exact-boundary + fast-track + FR-L3-precedes-fast-track, determinism, exactly-3-assignment, deterministic-same-post, different-post-different-sets, handles-never-raw-hashes, empty-pool-safe, blindedHandleFor-deterministic), 8 wire-codec (round-trip all decisions / identity-free-frame / wire names / unknown-decision-rejects / version-rejects / non-object-rejects / deterministic), 9 sink (local-first / single-sealed-enqueue / **byte-level sealed proof** / UUID-v4-idempotency / overwrite-reviews / localDecisions-snapshot / row-codec-round-trip / strict-bounds / failing-local-persist-no-partial-enqueue), 2 end-to-end integration (bloc submit → local record → sealed queue → **cold-restart row round-trip** → sync-drain byte-identical; approve→reject queues two sealed envelopes with the right decisions), 4 repo applyReview (3/3-publishes / reject-flag-no-advance / published-immutable / missing-id-no-op), 7 review-bloc (queue projection newest-first + blinded handles / approve-consensus-reprojects / reject-flag-recorded / reviewed-by-me / failing-sink-degrades / non-PII-projection), 6 review-section widget (N/3 indicator / 3/3-PUBLISHED-hides-controls / approve-publishes-leaves-queue / reject-marks-REVIEWED / shadow-teaser / **no-PII-hash-tree-scan**), 4 schema/migration (peer_reviews columns + no-identity / v6→v7 upgrade / v7 rollback), 4 security checkpoint additions; `flutter analyze` **0 issues**; affected suites **519/519 green** (ledger + database + state, `--concurrency=1`); **FULL suite 1068/1068 green** (`flutter test --concurrency=1`)
- **SECURITY CHECKPOINT PASSED — reviewer identities are blinded by construction**: `assignReviewers` returns ONLY derived `reviewer_` display handles — raw 64-hex blind hashes are never returned, logged, or rendered (asserted in gate tests + widget tree scan); the sealed review frame carries only post_id + decision (asserted — no phone/blind/device/voter words); the `peer_reviews` table has NO identity column by design (schema assertion); review production files import no `dart:io`/`package:http`/websockets and print nothing (static scan of all 6 new review files); the review section **defensively re-blinds** any hash-shaped author handle before it reaches the widget tree (proven by a widget test seeding a raw 64-hex handle)
- **Decisions & notes:** reviewer assignment is DETERMINISTIC client-side (seeded by post id — stable across devices, no split-brain); the actual reviewer pool is a server-side concern (the client renders blinded handles so the UI shows *a reviewer acted* without ever revealing *who*); reject/flag decisions are recorded + sealed-enqueued (moderation signal) but never advance the consensus; a post reaching 3/3 publishes locally and LEAVES the review queue (the section empties — no stale card); the gate's shadow/fast-track rules are pure functions (injectable inputs, no wall-clock, no randomness) so every device converges; the `[✓ Peer Review Gate: N/3 approved]` indicator mirrors DESIGN.md §7.2 and the existing detail-screen badge
- **PHASE 7 COMPLETE (7.1–7.6):** Task 7.6 complete — next: **Phase 8 Task 8.1 War Room UI Foundation**

### 2026-08-10 — Task 7.5: Voting System with Karma Weighting
- **Domain layer** — `LedgerVoteDirection` (up/down/none + strict wire names + per-direction deltas), `LedgerVote` (postId + direction), `LedgerVoteRecord` (durable local row: postId + direction + updatedAt), `LedgerVoteSink` port (save + localVotes recovery snapshot), `LedgerVoteWireFrame` + strict `encode/decodeLedgerVoteFrame` (v:1, ONLY post_id + direction — NO identity, NO PII, NO voter data; strict direction/version decode bounds); `KarmaWeightedScore` — the deterministic **sub-linear** client-side scoring rule `floor(sqrt(upvotes)) − floor(sqrt(downvotes))` (diminishing returns: every extra vote adds less than the last; downvotes subtract through the same curve; zero/negative inputs clamp; the function accepts ONLY two integers so identity can never enter — SECURITY CHECKPOINT 7.5); `LedgerPost` gains `myVote` (the device's own vote direction, default none) + `copyWith(myVote:)`
- **Schema & migration** — `post_votes` table added to `AppSchema` (v6: post_id TEXT PK + direction TEXT + updated_at INTEGER — **no identity column by design**); `Migration` v6 up (`CREATE TABLE post_votes`) + down (`DROP TABLE post_votes`); `ledgerVoteRecordToRow`/`ledgerVoteRecordFromRow` codecs (microsecond-exact updated_at); `AppSchema.currentVersion` → 6
- **Data layer** — `QueueLedgerVoteSink` (production `LedgerVoteSink`): **offline-first write order** — 1) the vote record is written to the `post_votes` table (SQLCipher-encrypted DB) IMMEDIATELY, then 2) a `SyncOperationType.update` item is enqueued with the serialized vote frame — the Task 3.3 AES-256-GCM `QueuePayloadCipher` SEALS it before storage, and the item id is a UUID v4 idempotency key (Task 5.2/5.3); `localVotes()` is the cold-restart recovery snapshot; a failing local persist propagates with NO silent partial enqueue; re-voting a post overwrites the record and queues another sealed envelope. `InMemoryLedgerFeedRepository.vote` is now **direction-based with toggle semantics**: same-direction tap removes the vote, switching directions moves by 2, unknown ids are no-ops, and downvotes are fully reversible from zero (the old 0-clamp broke invertibility — fixed: net tally may go negative, the karma display clamps at the projection layer)
- **State layer** — `LedgerFeedBloc.vote(postId, direction)` — the repository returns the **RESOLVED direction** (none when the same direction toggled off) and the sink enqueues that resolved state, so the sync envelope always carries the actual vote state, never the raw tap; `LedgerPostSummary` gains `myVote` + `karmaScore` (the deterministic sub-linear projection); a failing vote sink **degrades gracefully** — the vote stays applied locally and the feed re-emits, never a crash, never a rollback
- **UI layer** — shared `LedgerVoteBar` widget (up arrow / karma-weighted score / down arrow, ACTIVE-direction states in verifiedEmerald/alertRed with muted inactive arrows, compact feed-card variant) wired into BOTH the `LedgerFeedScreen` cards (interactive, threads `onVote` through the bloc) and `LedgerPostDetailScreen` (replaces the old read-only `_VoteButton` pair; optimistic local toggle mirrors the repository)
- VERIFY PASSED: **1009 Flutter tests total (45 new)** — 9 karma-score unit (zero/determinism/sub-linear-up/down-curve/net-difference/clamp/never-exceeds-raw/privacy-signature-of-two-ints/ofNet projection), 6 vote-wire-codec (round-trip all directions/identity-free-frame/unknown-direction-rejects/version-rejects/non-object-rejects/deterministic), 9 vote-sink (local-first/single-sealed-enqueue/**byte-level sealed proof**/UUID-v4-idempotency/overwrite-revotes/localVotes-snapshot/row-codec-round-trip/strict-bounds/failing-local-persist-no-partial-enqueue), 2 end-to-end integration (bloc vote → local record → sealed queue → **cold-restart row round-trip** → sync-drain byte-identical; toggle chain up→off→down queues three sealed envelopes with RESOLVED directions), 7 vote-bar widget (score/up-tap/down-tap/active-up/active-down/both-muted/score+arrows-only-no-identity), 2 feed-bloc (toggle-off re-emits + failing-sink-degrades), 1 repo (downvotes-reversible-from-zero), 2 detail-screen (same-direction-toggle-off + downvote-active-state), 1 schema (post_votes columns/no-identity), 2 migration (v5→v6 upgrade + v6 rollback), 4 security checkpoint additions; `flutter analyze` **0 issues**; affected suites **674/674 green** (run `--concurrency=1`; the parallel-run Argon2id checkpoint contention is pre-existing and unrelated); **FULL suite 1009/1009 green** (`flutter test --concurrency=1`)
- **SECURITY CHECKPOINT PASSED** — **vote weights ARE calculated client-side and CANNOT touch identity**: the scoring function's signature accepts only two integers (structural proof — there is no way to pass a voter identity, blind hash, or per-voter karma value); the sealed vote frame carries only post_id + direction (asserted — no author/phone/blind/voter words); the `post_votes` table has NO identity column by design; vote production files import no `dart:io`/`package:http` and print nothing (static scan); the vote bar renders only the karma score + arrows, never raw tallies
- **Decisions & notes:** the master plan's "vote caching in Redis with batch flush to PostgreSQL" is the SERVER-side aggregation (Phase 4.5/4.6 stack) — this task establishes the client contract: sealed vote envelopes the transport drains + the deterministic client-side score projection; the score is `floor(sqrt(up)) − floor(sqrt(down))` (sub-linear in both directions) so tallies cannot be weaponized into runaway scores; the queue envelope carries the RESOLVED post-toggle state (none on un-vote), not the raw tap, so the server applies the actual state; the old 0-floor clamp was removed from vote math because it made toggles non-invertible (a downvote at 0 then un-vote drifted to +1) — the karma display clamps negatives at the projection layer instead
- **PHASE 7 IN PROGRESS:** Task 7.5 complete — next: Task 7.6 Peer Review Gate

- **SECURITY CHECKPOINT PASSED** — **posts are encrypted before sync by construction**: the enqueue path seals the frame before the queue store sees it (byte-level proof in tests: stored payload ≠ plaintext frame bytes, opens back to the exact civic frame; queue store text scan finds no headline/body/pin plaintext); the wire frame carries zero identity fields (asserted); the draft row lives inside the SQLCipher-encrypted database with the coarse pin scope explicitly flagged sensitive; new production files import no `dart:io`/`package:http` and print nothing (static scan)
- **Decisions & notes:** per master-plan wording "Hive" is implemented as the `ledger_drafts` table in the existing SQLCipher DB (the app's established encrypted-at-rest store — no new storage engine); draft records carry public civic fields only and are NOT additionally sealed row-by-row (the whole file is SQLCipher-encrypted; only the queued envelope gets the queue-cipher seal because it crosses the sync boundary); the sync transport wiring (draining ledger posts to the API Gateway) follows in the later Phase 7 sync task — this task establishes the sealed-envelope contract the transport drains; queue + draft ids are independent (queue generates its own UUID v4 inside `enqueue`)

### 2026-08-10 — Task 7.3: Dynamic Radius UI (skill: dynamic-radius-ui)
- **Domain layer** — `LedgerPost` gains a coarse `district` field (public civic info, the constituency-fallback key — posts with no district are simply ineligible for expansion, a safe default); `FeedScope` value object (exact `pinCode` + coarse `ExploreRadius` enum + injectable `now` clock for the 7-day window + tunable `expansionThreshold` = 5); `FeedScopeResult` (blended `posts`, `expanded`, `nearbyCount`, explicit `nearbyIds` set — no ordering-based inference)
- **Data layer** — `LedgerFeedRepository.listScoped(FeedScope)` + `InMemoryLedgerFeedRepository` implementation of the dynamic-radius skill: **step 1 threshold check** (posts for the exact pin in the last 7 days via the injectable clock; FEWER than 5 → expansion eligible; stale >7-day posts never count toward the threshold but still render); **step 2 radius expansion** (same-district fallback — pin → district via the district carried by the pin's own posts; nearby posts collected with the category filter applied to BOTH sides); **step 4 seamless blending** (ONE chronological feed — local + nearby interleave by the standard status-first/newest-first order, no separate Local/Nearby sections); the result flags exactly which posts are nearby (`nearbyIds`)
- **State layer** — `LedgerFeedBloc.setRadius(ExploreRadius)` + `LocalLedgerFeedBloc` scope-aware `_emit`: the feed now reads through `listScoped`, carries `radius`/`isExpanded`/`nearbyCount` on `LedgerFeedState`, and maps posts via `LedgerPostSummary.from(post, nearby:)`; `LedgerPostSummary` gains the UI-safe `nearby` flag; `LedgerFeedState` gains `radius`/`isExpanded`/`nearbyCount`
- **UI layer** — `NearbyBadgeWidget` (fixed `NEARBY` label chip in Civic Gold — skill step 3, renders ONLY the label: no pin, no district, no identity); `LedgerFeedScreen` renders the badge on exactly the nearby cards, a `~ N posts from nearby pins ~` italic strip under the filter row when expanded, AND **relays the geo bloc's radius to the feed bloc** (a `StreamSubscription` on `geoBloc.state` calling `feedBloc.setRadius(radius)`, cancelled on dispose) — so a radius picked in `ExploreNearbySheet` immediately refetches the expanded feed end-to-end

- **SECURITY CHECKPOINT PASSED** — expanded results are clearly marked (NearbyBadge on exactly the expanded cards) AND the expansion adds zero location data: `FeedScope` carries only a pin + a coarse radius enum (no meters, no coordinates — asserted), the badge renders only the fixed `NEARBY` label (no pin/district/identity literals in source — scanned), the dynamic-radius files import no `dart:io`/`package:http` and print nothing, no PII-shaped literals anywhere in the new surface
- **Decisions & notes:** the expansion stays a pure LOCAL cache read (offline-first — no network, no server PostGIS call in this task; the wire contract from Task 7.2 remains for the sync work); the district is derived from the pin's own posts rather than a separate mapping table, so expansion is impossible (safe) when the cache has no district signal; the threshold/recency window are injectable so the skill's exact numbers (5 posts / 7 days) are both the default and testable; `ExploreRadius` 5/10/25 km choices currently map to the same district-level fallback — the graduated radii land with the server-side clustering

### 2026-08-10 — Task 7.2: Geographic Clustering Implementation
- **Domain layer (`lib/geo/domain/`)** — `PinCode` value object (strict `^[1-9]\d{5}$` validation, trims input, rejects leading-zero/non-digit/long-short values, coarse `circlePrefix` for district fallback queries, `forTest` fixture factory); `GeoPlace` (pin + district + locality names only, DESIGN §7.2 `scopeLine` = `District · Locality · 800001`) — **the ONLY location-bearing value that crosses the data-layer boundary**; `PinCodeResolution` + `PinCodeResolutionSource` (location/manual/persisted); `PinCodeResolver` port (throws `PinCodeResolutionException` on denial/unavailable/missing-postal); `PinCodeStore` port (persists ONLY the coarse place); `ExploreRadius` enum (Local only / 5 km / 10 km / 25 km with labels + scope hints)
- **Data layer (`lib/geo/data/`)** — `GeolocatorPinCodeResolver`: **the single file in the app that touches raw device coordinates**. Flow: `checkPermission` (requests `whileInUse` on denial) → `getCurrentPosition` (coarse `LocationAccuracy.low`, 8s time limit, falls back to last-known on timeout) → `placemarkFromCoordinates` → collapse to a coarse `GeoPlace` (postal → pin; `subAdministrativeArea` → district; `locality` kept; street/sub-locality DISCARDED). Coordinates are never returned, logged, stored, or uploaded — the pin code is the finest signal the rest of the app sees. Test-injectable `GeoLocationService`/`GeoCodingService` seams wrap the plugin statics; `LocalPinCodeStore` in-memory implementation
- **State layer** — `LedgerGeoBloc`/`LedgerGeoState` (`idle/resolving/resolved/manualEntryRequired`, coarse `place`, generic `hasError`, `ExploreRadius radius`) + `LocalLedgerGeoBloc`: resolution order is **persisted pin first (offline-first — zero location round-trips on cold start) → device location → generic manual-entry fallback**; monotonic sequence drops stale resolutions; the resolved place is persisted through `PinCodeStore`; any resolver failure (permission denied, service off, parse failure, unexpected exception) degrades to `manualEntryRequired` with a generic flag — never a crash, never reason-specific detail
- **UI** — `ExploreNearbySheet` (EXPLORE NEARBY title, coarse scope line, the four radius chips with selected-check state, `Use my location` re-resolve via `retryResolve`); wired into `LedgerFeedScreen` through a new `_MastheadWithExplore` radar chip: the masthead row shows the resolved `Sadar · Patna · 800001` scope line and tapping `Explore Nearby` opens the sheet, popping with the chosen `ExploreRadius` → `onRadiusChanged`. The feed's pin-code filtering reuses the Task 7.1 `listPosts(pinCode:)` scoping (verified by the geo-driven integration test); the geo bloc starts in the screen's `initState` AFTER the StreamBuilder subscribes (broadcast streams don't replay — the resolved scope line must be emitted post-subscription, proven by the integration test)
- VERIFY PASSED: **922 Flutter tests total (37 new)** — 7 PinCode validation (incl. the no-finer-than-pin proof: lat/lng/address strings are rejected), 4 GeoPlace, 7 geo bloc (persisted-pin-first w/ zero resolver calls, location resolve + persist, permission-degrade, unexpected-error-degrade, manual pin, radius expand/narrow, retry), 8 resolver (coarse-collapse strips street detail, permission-upgrade on request, deniedForever → exception, position-timeout → last-known fallback, missing position, malformed postal, empty placemark, plugin seam wiring), 4 ExploreNearbySheet widget (scope line + all radii, radius select pops the value through the modal, re-resolve calls retryResolve, **no coordinate-shaped text in the tree**), 2 feed geo integration (resolved scope line + sheet round trip + `onRadiusChanged`, control absent without a geo bloc), 5 security checkpoint; `flutter analyze` **0 issues**; affected suites **361/361 green** (37 geo + 324 ledger/state/ui — no regressions)
- **SECURITY CHECKPOINT PASSED — location is never a fingerprint vector**: (1) static scan proves only `geolocator_pin_code_resolver.dart` imports the `geolocator`/`geocoding` plugins — no other geo file can touch location APIs; (2) structural proof the domain + state declare NO coordinate-typed fields (latitude/longitude/accuracy/Position appear only in contract doc-comments); (3) `GeoPlace` is the sole cross-boundary location type and is coarse by construction (6-digit pin + civic names — no device id, no coordinate history, no timing correlation); (4) no `print(`/`debugPrint(` in any geo/ledger production file (raw coordinates can never leak to logs); (5) no phone/hash literals in the geo UI surface
- **Decisions & notes:** the server-side PostGIS constituency/district query stays on the Phase 4.5 PostgreSQL stack — this task establishes the client `PinCodeResolver` port + wire contract (`GeoPlace` = pin/district/locality) that the sync task invokes; `geolocator` 11.1.0 + `geocoding` 3.0.0 were already pinned in pubspec (no new deps); permission is `whileInUse`-only (the Ledger is foreground-scoped — background location would be both unnecessary and a fingerprinting risk); `LocationAccuracy.low` keeps the fix coarse by design; the radius control is deliberately UI-state-only today — the dynamic-radius feed expansion is Task 7.3 (skill: dynamic-radius-ui)
